# Scribing — recipe candidates

**40 cards; reviewed authored-operational PLAYTEST with AEX-01–07 fulfilled.** Sources and original book prose remain preserved.

## Refined Storm Tome — recipe-471

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

A completed blank carrier can encode one authentic, completed operational spell/ritual of rank at most 1; it allows 1 lifetime legal cast start(s), always of that same payload, plus +1 relevant research. Record exact spell/version/rank/class placement and genuine source access. A trained Calligrapher uses INT, the real desk/kit, max(1, rank) active hours, that many sp and 0.01 lb per hour of actual encoding supplies, and DC 10 + rank Core task binary resolution. Failure consumes time/supplies; encoding earns no crafting XP or Forge Dwarf reduction. No overwrite, capacity multiplication or refill. SC-03 expressly adds one-cast temporary repertoire only for a reader with the actual class/list, level/rank, RP, component and other spell eligibility. Use the spell's own action/time, range, concentration and cadence; pay one script use and normal resources at cast commitment. Counterspell can stop that paid cast; ready/release creates no second cast. The readable open script must be handled or supported within 5 feet; spell hands/components remain mandatory. Copying words does not copy magical uses. The research bonus requires the actual relevant lead consultation under SC-05. SC-01–05 and SC-10 govern the full procedure. These research/navigation bonuses apply only to their genuinely relevant recorded subject and actual eligible information task. They do not improve attacks, saves, spell DCs, spellcasting, unrelated checks, manufacture or repair. The item's own authoring, copying or encoding, and creation of a new bonus-granting reference from that same item, receive no self-production bonus. Genuine relevant formula research remains separately eligible without guaranteeing discovery.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: SC-book: Existing Book reference, NEW 200 writable pages/12x9x3-inch binding. No supplied factual content. 25 gp, 5 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 15, dimensions 12 × 9 × 3 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 6 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Scribing level 4; INT with actual Calligrapher's supplies training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 10. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 4 hours; operational 240 active minutes, or 180 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 225/165 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Scribe's Desk, with Calligrapher's supplies, Quill, Straightedge; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=expedition. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["trainer", "starter manual", "common experimentation"], "minimumResearchHours": 0, "requiresFormula": false, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 9, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 2; Standard 4; Exceptional yield 5; Masterwork yield 6. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 4 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Exact component occurrences**

All listed components are for the original whole batch, not per output. Repeated catalog IDs remain separate supplied quantities.

- [0] Preservation treatment (ing-471-0-preservation_treatment): quantity 1; tier 2; source unit 8 gp / 0.1 lb; role stabilizer; consumed=true.
- [1] sparkling petal (drop-sparkling_petal): quantity 1; tier 1; source unit 10 gp / 0.36 lb; role material; consumed=true.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

SC-book: finished unit 163/4 gp, functional repair material 25/4 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 163/16 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 10. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Unchanged source effect**

Creates a prepared text capable of storing one spell or ritual of up to 1st level; when used for research, grants +1 to the relevant Arcana, History, Religion, Nature, or Engineering check.

## Refined Tide Diagram — recipe-472

**Reviewed internal playtest; public; authored-operational exact approval complete.**

Use the [complete operating guide](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_OPERATIONAL_GUIDE.md) and [body catalogue](ADVANCED_CRAFT_INTERNAL_BODY_CATALOGUE.md).

**Effect and operation**

Actual authenticated map/diagram content grants +1 appropriate navigation and +1 relevant research through equipment.leather-task-enhancement. The lead must genuinely consult it for at least one necessary minute or throughout an independently allowed shorter task; its mapped place/subject must apply. Each physical item has 1 reroll per genuine formal expedition: after one related failed check but before consequences, reroll its original die selection with the same DC/modifiers/context and accept the replacement even if worse. One Scribing reroll per check; borrowing, repair, Watch changes and brief Haven stops do not reset it. Only formal prior expedition closure and a genuinely authorized new expedition provide another entitlement. Copies convey information, not the original item's magic or budget. SC-04–06 and SC-10 apply; an invented location or closed stowed map gives no benefit.

Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Scribing level 2; INT with actual Calligrapher's supplies training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 10. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 2 hours; operational 120 active minutes, or 90 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 105/75 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 0 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 2; Standard 4; Exceptional yield 5; Masterwork yield 6. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 4 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. **Effect and operation**

This is the custom multilingual research Lexicon. Its three Insight are reader/evidence scoped, not charges or fluency. Use the full SC-08 procedure below.

**Complete custom procedure — SC-08**

The supplied governing files mention translation progress but **do not supply a complete Language Insight currency/spend procedure**. Complete that specific gap here; do not label these numbers as recovered rules or invent an entire language-learning system.

**NEW Language Insight ledger:** points are recorded per researcher and one identified target language, maximum **six unspent points per language**. After **one active hour** of genuine study of a legible, authenticated, useful parallel text into a language that researcher understands,498/500 may grant their one point;674 grants **three** toward its one preregistered actual ancient language. The same underlying evidence/correspondences grant their points **once per researcher**, shared across original/copies, other physical editions and magically remade books using the same content. The reader/underlying evidence ID, not the current holder ID, is the anti-repeat key. Different readers can learn from the same real work; points cannot be transferred or sold as another character's comprehension. Saturation gives no substitute reward or later deferred excess.

**NEW finite use:** spend **three Insight for that language** and **eight active hours** of actual study with the evidence to translate one preregistered passage of at most **250 words** into a language the researcher knows. The passage must genuinely exist, be accessible/legible, and be covered by the evidence's actual vocabulary/correspondence; the Director must possess or establish the real source meaning. It grants that passage's supported literal translation, not general spoken fluency, hidden truths, deciphering a separate cipher or permission to learn a spell. Missing or unsupported meaning leaves the project unqualified before spending; it is not solved by a fabricated Insight result. This is a new specific translation procedure, not a reset of ordinary research progress or automatic completion of an unrelated project. The eight hours are work, not rest or Forge Dwarf creation. Use this specific Insight translation route or an independently authorized ordinary Translate project; the same active hours do not also award a second project's progress, an Insight gain or recipe XP.

674's **+3 cross-disciplinary research** applies only to genuine research requiring at least two of its printed five disciplines, with one declared lead check using the relevant actual skill and the normal assistance/proficiency rules. An eight-hour investigation at **NEW DC20** may examine an actually recorded ordinary cross-disciplinary correspondence: Core success reveals only the supported relation, failure no reliable new relation, critical no invented lore. Another attempt needs new evidence/method. It neither creates secret content nor operates its separate latent Matrix metadata. Its three points are not three new languages, per-day points or a spellcasting bonus.

This completes usable Insight behavior without approving the pending language feats. Additional ordinary fluency courses remain outside the recipe's promise, not a blocker to authoring674.

This is the custom one-bargain Codex. Consent, clauses, evidence, each signatory’s finite breach event and the independent mark deadline use the complete SC-09 procedure below.

**Complete custom procedure — SC-09**

KEEP one bargain, willing creatures, **6d10 psychic** breach and **30-day mark**; no spell/save/DC/charge field is silently added to the source. **NEW operating limits:** one lifetime bargain in this physical Codex, **two to six capable willing signatories**, at most **three objective clauses**, one specified start and deadline no later than **30 elapsed days** after final signature. All parties must be present within5ft, personally comprehend the entire text and consequences, and freely assent without compulsion, coercion, deception about the terms or inability to consent. Spend **ten active minutes** of actual reading/signing with the complete accessible unsuppressed Codex. Only the final legal signature commits its one bargain. A corpse, ordinary low-INT automaton, name without an actual signer, proxy or forged signature cannot consent.

Before commitment fix the exact parties, permitted actions/omissions, observability/evidence, deadline, impossibility exceptions and resolution order. Ambiguous, internally contradictory, inherently impossible, secretly changing or belief/emotion-detection clauses are not eligible. The codex is not an oracle, truth spell, ownership transfer, universal legal court or soul contract. Subsequent unavoidable impossibility not caused by a party's voluntary breach excuses that party's impossible performance; disputed facts require actual Director adjudication of the preregistered objective terms, not an unsupported caller boolean. Ordinary legal effects and fictional enforceability remain separate from this one agreed magical consequence.

**NEW enforcement range:** a breach can trigger only while the original operative Codex and that actual signatory are within one mile in the same established campaign world, with their actual locations recorded. The effect does not require visual contact, but it supplies no detection, evidence gathering or planar link; the pre-agreed objective facts still require genuine adjudication. Outside that scope there is no trigger or queued later punishment for that past event. A later different proven breach within scope may spend an otherwise unused entitlement. An already applied mark retains its own30-day deadline outside the range. Moving the Codex does not restart covenant or mark clocks.

**First proven voluntary breach by each signatory while the covenant is active and within that enforcement scope** spends that signatory's one breach entitlement, inflicts6d10 psychic through actual defenses as one nonspell magical damage event, and applies the mark even if damage is fully prevented. No save is added to a willingly accepted consequence. Repeated breaches, multiple clauses, repairs and extra turns cannot deal it again to that signer. At most six such events per Codex. The mark is a visible magical identifying sigil on one agreed physically possible patch of the violator's actual body or shell, selected at signing, not on a removable carried identification plate. Clothing can conceal it, but discarding clothing does not remove it. A later permitted form change preserves that same creature's mark and remaining deadline without granting any remote identification or tracking power. It supplies no tracking, compelled behavior, damage pulse, penalties, forced allegiance or extra target vulnerability. It is not automatically the generic Marked condition or a curse by name.

The mark expires30 elapsed days after its actual application. Earlier consensual release requires the original functional Codex, all surviving competent signatories' informed assent and ten active minutes; it ends future enforcement and its still-active marks, never refunds use or damage. An independently reviewed effect explicitly able to remove this kind of nonspell magical mark may apply within its real scope; no invented spell rank is assigned for a rank-limited dispel. Otherwise use release/expiry, not an unknown-tier automatic cure.

Enforcement follows only events in the same established campaign world/identity history; this rule creates no planar reach, cross-world tracking or cosmological transport. Suppression, unreadable/incomplete primary pages or Broken state suspends **future** enforcement while duration runs. Missed breaches are not queued or replayed on repair. Irrecoverable destruction ends future enforcement; marks already applied retain their own duration/removal. Fulfillment/deadline ends future enforcement, not already applied marks. A copy cannot enforce another covenant or restore a spent one.

**NEW ordinary-annotation reader gate:** a researcher of character level11 or higher with actual proficiency in at least three of Arcana/History/Religion/Nature/Engineering may spend one active hour studying675 to perceive its one actually provided ordinary hidden annotation. This is one bounded local reading, not automatically the Matrix clue in its separately preserved metadata. With no actual authorized annotation content, reveal no invented message. The latent Matrix object and literal wording remain preserved and nonoperative pending their own later gate.

**Complete output bodies**

Choose before any work: SC-book: Existing Book reference, NEW 200 writable pages/12x9x3-inch binding. No supplied factual content. 25 gp, 5 lb, ordinary B5, structural AC 15, dimensions 12 × 9 × 3 inches. See the complete body catalogue for original primaries, constituent masses, hands, capacity and support. A creature output replaces stock object HP/AC with its printed creature statistics. EN alternate weapon/suit hosts use their exact registered existing equipment profile; these default figures do not overwrite that alternate body.

**Before creation**

Reserve 1 actual complete never-magically-altered bodies, one model/size per order, in an exclusive fixed order. All must be complete and positive-HP immediately before the completion roll, even if fewer outputs will result. Bodies are additional to the exact source batch components and working tools. Pre-roll lawful replacement keeps its ordered position; no post-roll acquisition, substitution or reordering. Transform the first resolved outputs; unused bodies remain actual mundane stock. Record material identity, condition, real storage, actual station capacity, preservation and safe handling; a quoted price supplies none of those facts.

**Creation check and work**

Scribing level 20; INT with actual Calligrapher's supplies training, one PB or replacing expertise, DC 28. Untrained otherwise eligible level 1–4 uses no tool PB and disadvantage; trained recipe eligibility is max(4, trade level), with actual formula access. Source duration 3 months; operational 28800 active minutes, or 21600 for an eligible Forge Dwarf. The included first stable phase is 15 minutes (15 for that Dwarf); remaining work is 28785/21585 minutes respectively, in declared phases of at most 480 minutes. Only actual incorporated inputs stabilize; raw/returned catalyst clocks continue. Interruption loses unfinished-phase progress, not costs or completed work.

**Facility and assistance**

Actual Scribe's Desk, with Calligrapher's supplies, Quill, Straightedge; fieldCraftable=false, source timeMode=legacy. A field label never overrides the false creation flag. Fixed compatible facilities, actual minimum surface/bay/fixtures, safe access and expedition-site permissions remain required. Maximum 3 eligible assistants work all required hours without shortening the task or gaining assistant XP. Ordinary facilities/tools grant +0. Two active work Watches, no overlapping labor/rest credit, actual hired capacity and lawful access still apply.

**Formula and advancement**

Preserve the exact discovery and progression records: {"discovery": {"sources": ["sealed library", "world event", "Gate Guardian cache", "multiseason research"], "minimumResearchHours": 48, "requiresFormula": true, "formulaConsumed": false}, "skillProgression": {"skillXPOnSuccess": 4, "trivialAtSkillLevel": 20, "firstCraftBonus": true}}. The crafting framework determines actual first/repeat/qualified-failure/triviality XP. Source skillXPOnSuccess is not an extra award on use, repair, study, service or temporary class fabrication. A finished item supplies no formula/proficiency.

**Manufacturing outcomes**

Mishap 0; Failed 0; Flawed 1; Standard 1; Exceptional yield 1; Masterwork yield 1. Natural 1 is Mishap; natural 20 adds no band shift. An eligible Forge Dwarf's Standard becomes Exceptional. Alternatively retain 1 structural objects with the strongest P25/P50 workmanship once. Maximum = B + max(1, floor(B×P/100)) for positive P, else B; one existing/new singleton persistent flaw halves last, minimum 1. Preserve old maximum minus current damage deficit, clamping the new current to [0,newMax]. Q1 Flawed creates one persistent flaw; Q≥2 reduced yield adds no new singleton flaw, even when one output remains. Standard never heals. Actual EN host size/grade B takes precedence over default-body examples. No outcome improves charges, potency, source DC, spell rank, capacities or permissions; later repair does not remove a flaw or reapply manufacturing quality.

**Retention and failure**

This recipe has no eligible catalyst-return occurrence. On Failed creation, for each eligible exact consumed non-catalyst input identity, recover at most one quarter of its fixed consumed-input quantity for this batch, capped by the eligible amount actually remaining from those same inputs, and rounded down once to that identity's legal source-unit precision. Do not take one quarter of the surviving remainder. Previously returned, forfeited or lost quantities and unrelated stock cannot fund this recovery. For each exact eligible identity in this batch, use `recovered_i = u_i × floor(min(Q_i / 4, R_i) / u_i)`. Q_i is its fixed consumed-input quantity; R_i is the eligible amount surviving from those same inputs; u_i is its legal source-unit precision. Group only genuinely identical eligible inputs within this same batch while retaining every physical occurrence. Catalysts and unrelated batches do not enter that recovery group. The per-occurrence record identifies only expressly divisible bulk; otherwise floor to indivisible units. No quarter tool/lens/core, money conversion, duplicate catalyst, or second salvage pass over waste. Mishap/abandonment returns no consumed inputs. Retained working tools and unused complete bodies survive only as they actually exist. Returned material retains real age, vessels and history; a retained catalyst is not also inside the finished item.

**Restoration and reference prices**

SC-book: finished unit 88259 gp, functional repair material 25/4 gp and default 1 hours at DC 10; recoverable Broken/wreck reconstruction material 88259/4 gp and default 8 hours at original DC 28. Recompute actual time from current recorded maximum HP: max(1, maxHP/10) or max(8, maxHP/10). Alternate admitted host price/size/grade uses its actual reference. Trained primary trade + INT, original station/implements and host-compatible repair fixtures are required. Original creation record and all magic-bearing primaries must survive; missing/burnt pages, missing model-designated primary frame/actuator, sacrificed or already-consumed debris are not reconstructible by payment. Core task natural shifts collapse to success/failure; failure spends time/material. Restore only recorded HP and lawful function, never charges/service/spell uses, flaws or prior deadlines. No Forge reduction, quality, extra generic assistants or XP. Reference = original batch market value / original Q + actual body; keep exact fractions until final copper purchase-ceiling/sale-floor. Actual quotes, availability, service and ownership are separate.

**State and interaction**

Initialize each actual output’s budget once at completion. Track physical identity, controller/holder, complete structure, current HP, suppression/meld, permissions, exact clock/expedition/round events and expenditures. Only actual legal commitment spends resources; legal committed misses/prevention/interruption do not refund them. No transfers, snapshots, copies, repair or relabeling reset state. Use the complete shared guide alongside this card. Source titles/donors/private research do not create targets, facts, custody, permissions or cosmology.

**Unchanged source effect**

Records and enforces one written bargain between willing creatures. Violating the bargain inflicts 6d10 psychic damage and marks the violator for 30 days. The codex also preserves one hidden annotation visible only to high-level researchers.
